, from (
E.149
), we can write

\displaystyle d_{1}\left(\frac{T_{N,2}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2},\mathbb{K}_{3})}{\sigma_{N,2}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2},\mathbb{K}_{3})},~Z\right)\leq O\left(\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}(1-\alpha)}}\right).

This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma D.18
.

Assume
F
and
G
are both cumulative distribution functions (cdf’s) on
\mathbb{R}
, with,
G(x)=\int_{-\infty}^{x}g(u)~du
, for each
x\in\mathbb{R}
, where
g(\cdot)
is a probability density function (pdf). Then,

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}}|F(x)-G(x)|=\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}}|F(x^{-})-G(x^{-})|,

where,
F(x^{-})=\lim_{h\rightarrow 0}F(x-h)
and
G(x^{-})
is also defined similarly.

Proof of Lemma
D.18
.

Firstly we establish uniform continuity of
G(\cdot)
. Fix
\epsilon>0
, we need to show that there exists a
\delta>0
, such that
|x-y|<\delta
, will imply
|G(x)-G(y)|<\epsilon
. Let
x<y
, with
(y-x)<\delta
. Note that,

\displaystyle|G(y)-G(x)|

\displaystyle=\int_{x}^{y}g(u)~du=\int_{x}^{y}g(u)\cdot\mathbf{1}\left(g(u)>M\right)~du+\int_{x}^{y}g(u)\cdot\mathbf{1}\left(g(u)\leq M\right)~du,

for any
M>0
, which is to be chosen later. As
g
is integrable, hence by DCT, there exists a
M_{\epsilon}>0
, such that

\int_{\mathbb{R}}g(u)\cdot\mathbf{1}(g(u)>M_{\epsilon})~du<\frac{\epsilon}{2},

where the choice of
M_{\epsilon}
does not depend on
x
and
y
, and only depends on
\epsilon
. Thus, with this choice of
M=M_{\epsilon}
, we obtain

|G(y)-G(x)|<\frac{\epsilon}{2}+M_{\epsilon}\cdot|y-x|\leq\frac{\epsilon}{2}+M_{\epsilon}\cdot\delta.

Choose,
\delta=\epsilon/(2M_{\epsilon})
. This leads to

G(y)-G(x)<\frac{\epsilon}{2}+\frac{\epsilon}{2}=\epsilon.

As
x
and
y
are arbitrary and
\delta
depends only on
\epsilon
, we have established uniform continuity of
G
. Let
X
and
Y
denote the underlying random variables corresponding to the cdf’s
F
and
G
respectively. Then for each
\epsilon>0
,

F(x^{-})=\mathbf{P}(X<x)\leq F(x)\leq\mathbf{P}(X<x+\epsilon)=F({(x+\epsilon)}^{-}).

Uniform continuity implies continuity,
i.e.
,
G(x^{-})=\lim_{h\rightarrow 0}G(x-h)=G(x)
. Write,
\Delta=\sup_{x}|F(x^{-})-G(x^{-})|
. Then,

\displaystyle F(x^{-})-G(x)\leq F(x)-G(x)\leq F({(x+\epsilon)}^{-})-G(x)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ -|F(x^{-})-G(x)|\leq F(x^{-})-G(x)\leq F(x)-G(x)\leq F({(x+\epsilon)}^{-})-G(x+\epsilon)+G(x+\epsilon)-G(x)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ -\Delta\leq F(x)-G(x)\leq\sup_{t}|F(t^{-})-G(t^{-})|+\sup_{t}|G(t+\epsilon)-G(t)|=\Delta+\Delta_{1,\epsilon}\hskip 8.50012pt\text{(say),}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ -\Delta-\Delta_{1,\epsilon}\leq-\Delta\leq F(x)-G(x)\leq\Delta+\Delta_{1,\epsilon},\hskip 8.50012pt\text{for all $x$ and all $\epsilon>0$,}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ \sup_{x}|F(x)-G(x)|\leq\Delta+\Delta_{1,\epsilon}.

As
G
is uniformly continuous, letting
\epsilon\downarrow 0
,
\Delta_{1,\epsilon}\rightarrow 0
. This implies,
\sup_{x}|F(x)-G(x)|\leq\Delta
. To show the reverse inequality, we write for each
x
and
\epsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}(X\leq x-\epsilon)\leq\mathbf{P}(X<x)\leq\mathbf{P}(X\leq x)\leq\mathbf{P}(X\leq x+\epsilon)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ F(x-\epsilon)\leq F(x^{-})\leq F(x+\epsilon)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ F(x-\epsilon)-G(x)\leq F(x^{-})-G(x)\leq F(x+\epsilon)-G(x)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ F(x-\epsilon)-G(x-\epsilon)+G(x-\epsilon)-G(x)\leq F(x^{-})-G(x)\leq F(x+\epsilon)-G(x+\epsilon)+G(x+\epsilon)-G(x)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ -\sup_{t}|F(t)-G(t)|-\sup_{t}|G(t-\epsilon)-G(t)|\leq F(x^{-})-G(x)\leq\sup_{t}|F(t)-G(t)|+\sup_{t}|G(t+\epsilon)-G(t)|

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ -\sup_{t}|F(t)-G(t)|-\Delta_{2,\epsilon}\leq F(x^{-})-G(x^{-})\leq\sup_{t}|F(t)-G(t)|+\Delta_{1,\epsilon}\hskip 8.50012pt\text{(say),}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ -\sup_{t}|F(t)-G(t)|-\Delta_{\epsilon}\leq F(x^{-})-G(x^{-})\leq\sup_{t}|F(t)-G(t)|+\Delta_{\epsilon},\hskip 8.50012pt\text{where $\Delta_{\epsilon}=\max\{\Delta_{1,\epsilon},\Delta_{2,\epsilon}\}$}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ \Delta=\sup_{x}|F(x^{-})-G(x^{-})|\leq\sup_{t}|F(t)-G(t)|+\Delta_{\epsilon},

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ \Delta\leq\sup_{x}|F(x)-G(x)|,\hskip 8.50012pt\text{as $\Delta_{\epsilon}\rightarrow 0$ when $\epsilon\downarrow 0$.}

This establishes the reverse inequality, indicating the equality of both suprema.
∎

Proposition D.19
.

Suppose that Assumptions
(A.1)
,
(A.2)
,
(A.3)
and
(A.4)
hold. Let
Z\sim N(0,1)
. For
H=\mathbb{K}_{1,2}
(wedge), consider the estimated wedge count
\widehat{S}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})
for
l=1,2
and define
\psi^{2}_{N,l}(H)=\mathbf{Var}\left(\widehat{S}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)
for
l=1,2
. Then

\displaystyle d_{1}\left(\frac{\widehat{S}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})-\mathbf{E}\left(\widehat{S}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)}{\psi_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})},~Z\right)\to 0,~\text{as}~N\to\infty,~\text{for $l=1,2$}.

Proof.

For
l=1,2
, define,

\displaystyle Z_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})

\displaystyle=\frac{\widehat{S}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})-\mathbf{E}\left(\widehat{S}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)}{\psi_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\psi_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})}\sum_{\mathbf{s}\in{[N]}_{R}}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})-\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\right).

Then, the variance of
\widehat{S}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})
can be written as follows.

\displaystyle\psi^{2}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})=\mathbf{Var}\left(\widehat{S}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)=\mathbf{E}\left(\left(\sum_{\mathbf{s}\in{[N]}_{R}}\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}:H)\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}:H)\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}-\left(\mathbf{E}\left(\sum_{\mathbf{s}\in{[N]}_{R}}\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}:H)\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}:H)\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}{[N]}_{R}}\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:H)\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:H)\right)\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:H)\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:H)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}-\left(\sum_{\mathbf{s}\in{[N]}_{R}}\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}:H)\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}:H)\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}{[N]}_{R}}\mathbf{E}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:H)\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:H)\right)\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:H)\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:H)\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}{[N]}_{R}}\mathbf{E}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\mathbf{E}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}-\left(\sum_{\mathbf{s}\in{[N]}_{R}}\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})+2\sum_{\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}{[N]}_{R}}\mathbf{E}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\mathbf{E}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\sum_{\mathbf{s}\in{[N]}_{R}}\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})+2f^{2}_{l}(p_{N}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\sum_{\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}{[N]}_{R}}\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}+f^{2}_{l}(p_{N}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\left(\sum_{\mathbf{s}\in{[N]}_{R}}\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\right)^{2}.

(E.157)

Therefore, using Lemma
D.5
and Lemma (
D.12
) (one can use exact expression of
\sigma^{2}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})
provided in (
E.159
) and (
E.160
)), for large enough
N
, we can write

\displaystyle\frac{\psi^{2}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})}{\sigma^{2}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})}=1+O\left(\frac{1}{N}\right).

Using the Theorem
C.1
over
Z_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})
, we can write

\displaystyle d_{1}\left(Z_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2}),~N(0,1)\right)\leq\frac{1}{\psi^{3}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}=1}^{3}\sum_{(\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2},\mathbf{s}_{3})\in\mathcal{M}_{t_{1},t_{2}}}\bigg\{\mathbf{E}\prod_{i=1}^{3}\bigg|\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{i}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{i}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}-\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{i}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{i}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\bigg|+\mathbf{E}\Bigg|\bigg\{\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})

\displaystyle-\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{1}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\bigg\}\bigg\{\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})-\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{2}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\right)\bigg\}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\times\mathbf{E}\left|\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{3}:\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{3})-\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{3})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{3})\right)\right|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma^{3}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})}{\psi^{3}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})}\frac{1}{\sigma^{3}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}=1}^{3}\sum_{(\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2},\mathbf{s}_{3})\in\mathcal{M}_{t_{1},t_{2}}}\Bigg(\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{1})\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{2})\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{3})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 17.00024pt\cdot\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{1})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{2})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{3})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt+\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{1})\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{2})\right)\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{1})\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{2})\right)\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{N,l}(\mathbf{s}_{3})\right)\mathbf{E}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{N}(\mathbf{s}_{3})\right)\Bigg).

The above bound is similar to the bound obtained in the proof of Theorem
3.1
and the upper bound goes to zero under similar assumption required in Theorem
3.1
and Corollary
3.2
. In addition, note that, since
\psi^{2}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})/\sigma^{2}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\to 1
as
N\rightarrow\infty
, then by Lemma
D.12
, as
N\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\frac{\psi^{2}_{N,l}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})}{N^{-g_{l,\alpha,\beta,H}(R)+g_{l,\alpha,\beta,H}(t_{l}(\alpha,\beta;H))}}\to\psi_{l}^{2}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})\in(0,\infty),~\text{for $l=1,2$.}

This completes the proof.

∎

Appendix E: Variance expressions for some common target subgraphs

We provide expression of
\sigma^{2}_{N,l}(H)
for some common target subgraphs, such as the edge, wedge, and triangle at any choice of
(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{l}(H)
(cf. (
1.6
) and (
1.7
)). Similar expressions for any complete and star graph are also provided. The expressions are provided in terms of the SBM edge probabilities
((\pi_{N,u,v}:1\leq u,v\leq K))
and recall
N^{\beta}\cdot\bm{\Pi}_{N}\asymp\mathbf{C}
, the class size proportions
\lambda_{N,1},\ldots,\lambda_{N,K}
and the node sampling probability
p_{N}=c/N^{\alpha}
. These variance expressions are exact and valid for all choices of
N
.

1.

Edge:

H=\mathbb{K}_{2}
. In this case,

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{N,1}(\mathbb{K}_{2})

\displaystyle=4\cdot N^{3}\cdot p_{N}^{3}(1-p_{N})\cdot\sum_{u,v,w=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,u,w}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w}

(E.158)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+2\cdot N^{2}\cdot p_{N}^{2}(1-p_{N}^{2})\sum_{u,v=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v},\hskip 8.50012pt\text{for $(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{1}(\mathbb{K}_{2})$ and}

\displaystyle\sigma_{N,2}^{2}(\mathbb{K}_{2})

\displaystyle=4\cdot N^{3}\cdot p_{N}(1-p_{N}^{3})\sum_{u,v,w=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,u,w}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+2\cdot N^{2}\cdot p_{N}(2-p_{N})(1-p_{N})^{2}\sum_{u,v=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v},\hskip 8.50012pt\text{for $(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{2}(\mathbb{K}_{2})$.}

2.

Wedge:

H=\mathbb{K}_{1,2}
. In this case,

\displaystyle\sigma_{N,1}^{2}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})

\displaystyle=N^{5}\cdot p_{N}^{5}(1-p_{N})\sum_{u,v,w,x,y=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,u,w}\cdot(\pi_{N,u,x}\pi_{N,u,y}+4\cdot\pi_{N,u,x}\pi_{N,x,y}

(E.159)

\displaystyle}{}{\displaystyle+4\cdot\pi_{N,v,x}\pi_{N,x,y})\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w}\lambda_{N,x}\lambda_{N,y}+

\displaystyle+N^{4}\cdot p_{N}^{4}(1-p_{N}^{2})\sum_{u,v,w,x=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,u,w}\cdot(4\cdot\pi_{N,u,x}+4\cdot\pi_{N,v,x}

\displaystyle}{}{\displaystyle+2\cdot\pi_{N,v,x}\pi_{N,u,x}+8\cdot\pi_{N,v,x}\pi_{N,w,x})\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w}\lambda_{N,x}+

\displaystyle+N^{3}\cdot p_{N}^{3}(1-p_{N}^{3})\sum_{u,v,w=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,u,w}\cdot(2+4\cdot\pi_{N,v,w})\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w},\hskip 8.50012pt\text{for $(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{1}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})$,}

and

\displaystyle\sigma_{N,2}^{2}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})

\displaystyle=N^{5}\cdot\sum_{u,v,w,x,y=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,u,w}\cdot\{p_{N}(1-p_{N})^{3}\pi_{N,u,x}\pi_{N,u,y}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}+4\cdot p_{N}^{2}(1-p_{N})^{3}(1+p_{N})\pi_{N,u,x}\pi_{N,x,y}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}+4\cdot p_{N}^{3}(1-p_{N})^{3}\pi_{N,v,x}\pi_{N,x,y}\}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w}\lambda_{N,x}\lambda_{N,y}

\displaystyle+N^{4}\sum_{u,v,w,x=1}^{N}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,u,w}\cdot\{4\cdot p_{N}(1-p_{N})^{2}(1+p_{N}-p_{N}^{3})\pi_{N,u,x}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}+4\cdot p_{N}^{2}(1-p_{N})(2-p_{N}^{2})\cdot\pi_{N,v,x}

\displaystyle+2\cdot p_{N}^{2}(1-p_{N})^{3}\pi_{v,x}\pi_{N,u,x}+8\cdot p_{N}^{2}(1-p_{N})^{3}(1+p_{N})\cdot\pi_{N,v,x}\pi_{N,w,x}\}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w}\lambda_{N,x}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}+N^{3}\sum_{u,v,w=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,u,w}\cdot\{2\cdot(p_{N}+(1-p_{N})p_{N}^{2})\cdot(1-(p_{N}+(1-p_{N})p_{N}^{2}))

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}+4\cdot p_{N}^{2}(1-p_{N})^{2}(2-p_{N}^{2})\cdot\pi_{N,v,w}\}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w},\hskip 8.50012pt\text{for $(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{2}(\mathbb{K}_{1,2})$.}

(E.160)

3.

Triangle:

H=\mathbb{K}_{3}
. In this case,

\displaystyle\sigma_{N,1}^{2}(\mathbb{K}_{3})

\displaystyle=9\cdot N^{5}\cdot p_{N}^{5}(1-p_{N})

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}\times\sum_{u,v,w,x,y=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,v,w}\pi_{N,w,u}\pi_{N,u,x}\pi_{N,x,y}\pi_{N,y,u}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w}\lambda_{N,x}\lambda_{N,y}+

\displaystyle+18\cdot N^{4}\cdot p_{N}^{4}(1-p_{N}^{2})\sum_{u,v,w,x=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,v,w}\pi_{N,w,u}\pi_{N,v,x}\pi_{N,x,u}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w}\lambda_{N,x}+

\displaystyle+6\cdot N^{3}\cdot p_{N}^{3}(1-p_{N}^{3})\sum_{u,v,w=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,v,w}\pi_{N,w,u}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w},\hskip 8.50012pt\text{for $(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{1}(\mathbb{K}_{3})$}

(E.161)

and

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{N,2}(\mathbb{K}_{3})

\displaystyle=36\cdot N^{5}\cdot p_{N}^{3}(1-p_{N})^{3}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}\times\sum_{u,v,w,x=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,v,w}\pi_{N,w,u}\pi_{N,u,x}\pi_{N,x,y}\pi_{N,y,u}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w}\lambda_{N,x}\lambda_{N,y}+

\displaystyle+18\cdot N^{4}\cdot(p_{N}^{2}+2p_{N}^{3}-2p_{N}^{4}-9p_{N}^{4}+12p_{N}^{5}-4p^{6})

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}\sum_{u,v,w,x=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,v,w}\pi_{N,w,u}\pi_{N,v,x}\pi_{N,x,u}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w}\lambda_{N,x}

\displaystyle+6\cdot N^{3}\cdot(3\cdot p_{N}^{2}-2\cdot p_{N}^{3})(1-(3\cdot p_{N}^{2}-2\cdot p_{N}^{3}))

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}\times\sum_{u,v,w=1}^{K}\pi_{N,u,v}\pi_{N,v,w}\pi_{N,w,u}\cdot\lambda_{N,u}\cdot\lambda_{N,v}\lambda_{N,w},\hskip 8.50012pt\text{for $(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{2}(\mathbb{K}_{3})$.}

(E.162)

4.

Star graph:

H=\mathbb{K}_{1,R-1}
, for any
R\geq 2
. Recall the definition of the sets
C_{l,j}(H)
,
l=1,2
,
j=1,\ldots,R
(cf. (
1.8
)). In this case, we obtain asymptotic expressions,

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{N,1}(\mathbb{K}_{1,R-1})

\displaystyle\asymp\begin{cases}N^{2R-1}p_{N}^{2R-1}(1-p_{N})\displaystyle\sum_{a,b=1}^{R}\sum_{\mathbf{u},\mathbf{v}\in[K]^{R}}\mathbf{1}(u_{a}=v_{b})\\[5.10011pt]
\hskip 17.00024pt\cdot\left(\prod_{j=2}^{R}\pi_{u_{1},u_{j}}\prod_{j=2}^{R}\pi_{v_{1},v_{j}}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{R}\lambda_{u_{j}}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq b\end{subarray}}^{R}\lambda_{v_{k}},\\[10.20012pt]
\hfill\text{when}\hskip 8.50012pt(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{1,1}(\mathbb{K}_{1,R-1}),\\[6.88889pt]
N^{R}p_{N}^{R}(1-p_{N})^{R}\,R\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{u}\in[K]^{R}}\left(\prod_{j=2}^{R}\pi_{u_{1},u_{j}}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{R}\lambda_{u_{j}},\\[10.20012pt]
\hfill\text{when}\hskip 8.50012pt(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{1,R}(\mathbb{K}_{1,R-1}).\end{cases}

(E.163)

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{N,2}(\mathbb{K}_{1,R-1})

\displaystyle\asymp\begin{cases}N^{2R-1}p_{N}\displaystyle\sum_{a,b=1}^{R}\sum_{\mathbf{u},\mathbf{v}\in[K]^{R}}\mathbf{1}(u_{a}=v_{b})\\[5.10011pt]
\hskip 17.00024pt\cdot\left(\prod_{j=2}^{R}\pi_{u_{1},u_{j}}\prod_{j=2}^{R}\pi_{v_{1},v_{j}}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{R}\lambda_{u_{j}}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq b\end{subarray}}^{R}\lambda_{v_{k}},\hskip 8.50012pt\text{when}\hskip 8.50012pt(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{2,1}(\mathbb{K}_{1,R-1}),\\[6.88889pt]
N^{R}(p_{N}+(1-p_{N})p_{N}^{R-1})(1-(p_{N}+(1-p_{N})p_{N}^{R-1}))\,(R-1)!\\
\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{u}\in[K]^{R}}\left(\prod_{j=2}^{R}\pi_{u_{1},u_{j}}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{R}\lambda_{u_{j}},\hskip 17.00024pt\text{when}\hskip 8.50012pt(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{2,R}(\mathbb{K}_{1,R-1}).\end{cases}

(E.164)

5.

Complete graph:

H=\mathbb{K}_{R}
, for any
R\geq 3
. Here,

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{N,1}(\mathbb{K}_{R})

\displaystyle\asymp\begin{cases}N^{2R-1}p_{N}^{2R-1}(1-p_{N})\displaystyle\sum_{a,b=1}^{R}\sum_{\mathbf{u},\mathbf{v}\in[K]^{R}}\mathbf{1}(u_{a}=v_{b})\\[5.10011pt]
\hskip 17.00024pt\cdot\left(\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq R}\pi_{u_{i},u_{j}}\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq R}\pi_{v_{i},v_{j}}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{R}\lambda_{u_{j}}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq b\end{subarray}}^{R}\lambda_{v_{k}},\\[10.20012pt]
\hfill\text{when}\hskip 8.50012pt(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{1,1}(\mathbb{K}_{R}),\\[6.88889pt]
N^{R}p_{N}^{R}(1-p_{N})^{R}\,R\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{u}\in[K]^{R}}\left(\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq R}\pi_{u_{1},u_{j}}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{R}\lambda_{u_{j}},\\[10.20012pt]
\hfill\text{when}\hskip 8.50012pt(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{1,R}(\mathbb{K}_{R}).\end{cases}

(E.165)

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{N,2}(\mathbb{K}_{R})

\displaystyle\asymp\begin{cases}N^{2R-1}p_{N}^{2R-3}\displaystyle\sum_{a,b=1}^{R}\sum_{\mathbf{u},\mathbf{v}\in[K]^{R}}\mathbf{1}(u_{a}=v_{b})\\[5.10011pt]
\hskip 17.00024pt\cdot\left(\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq R}\pi_{u_{i},u_{j}}\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq R}\pi_{v_{i},v_{j}}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{R}\lambda_{u_{j}}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq b\end{subarray}}^{R}\lambda_{v_{k}},\\[10.20012pt]
\hfill\text{when}\hskip 8.50012pt(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{2,1}(\mathbb{K}_{R}),\\[6.88889pt]
N^{2R-1}p_{N}^{R-1}\displaystyle\sum_{(\mathbf{j},\mathbf{k})\in J_{t}\cap M_{t}}\sum_{\xi\in\mathcal{S}_{t}}\sum_{\mathbf{u},\mathbf{v}\in[K]^{R}}\prod_{i=1}^{R-1}\mathbf{1}(u_{j_{i}}=v_{k_{\xi(i)}})\\[5.10011pt]
\hskip 17.00024pt\cdot\left(\prod_{\{i,j\}\in E(\mathbb{K}_{R})}\pi_{u_{i},u_{j}}\prod_{\{i,j\}\in E(\mathbb{K}_{R})\setminus B(\mathbf{k},R-1)}\pi_{v_{i},v_{j}}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{R}\lambda_{u_{j}}\prod_{k\in[R]\setminus A(\mathbf{k})}^{R}\lambda_{v_{k}},\\[10.20012pt]
\hfill\text{when}\hskip 8.50012pt(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{2,R-1}(\mathbb{K}_{R}),\\[6.88889pt]
N^{R}\left(R\cdot p_{N}^{R-1}(1-p_{N})+p_{N}^{R}\right)\left(1-\left(R\cdot p_{N}^{R-1}(1-p_{N})+p_{N}^{R}\right)\right)R!\\
\hskip 17.00024pt{}\hskip 17.00024pt{}\times\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{u}\in[K]^{R}}\left(\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq R}\pi_{u_{1},u_{j}}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{R}\lambda_{u_{j}},\\[10.20012pt]
\hfill\text{when}\hskip 8.50012pt(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{2,R}(\mathbb{K}_{R}).\end{cases}

(E.166)

6.

Line graph:

H=\mathbb{L}_{R}
. The variance expression is provided only for the induced case, since the line graph fails to satisfy Assumption
(A.5)
.

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{N,1}(\mathbb{L}_{R})

\displaystyle\asymp\begin{cases}N^{2R-1}p_{N}^{2R-1}(1-p_{N})\displaystyle\sum_{a,b=1}^{R}\sum_{\mathbf{u},\mathbf{v}\in[K]^{R}}\mathbf{1}(u_{a}=v_{b})\\[5.10011pt]
\hskip 17.00024pt\cdot\left(\prod_{j=1}^{R-1}\pi_{u_{j},u_{j+1}}\prod_{j=1}^{R-1}\pi_{v_{j},v_{j+1}}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{R}\lambda_{u_{j}}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq b\end{subarray}}^{R}\lambda_{v_{k}},\\[10.20012pt]
\hfill\text{when}\hskip 8.50012pt(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{1,1}(\mathbb{L}_{R}),\\[6.88889pt]
N^{R}p_{N}^{R}(1-p_{N})^{R}\,R\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{u}\in[K]^{R}}\left(\prod_{j=1}^{R-1}\pi_{u_{j},u_{j+1}}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{R}\lambda_{u_{j}},\\[10.20012pt]
\hfill\text{when}\hskip 8.50012pt(\alpha,\beta)\in C_{1,R}(\mathbb{L}_{R}).\end{cases}

(E.167)
